a:T5b5,The experiments cover OLMoE-1B-7B, DeepSeek-V2-Lite, and Moonlight-16B-A3B, all with 64-expert MoE layouts, plus Granite-3.0-3B-A800M for an additional router-normalization test. Pruning ratios include 25%, 50%, and 75%; at 50% pruning the paper also crosses four expert-selection criteria with the main replacement operators. The gate-scale effect is large. On routers trained without top-k renormalization, preserving the trained scale improves OLMoE by about 4.50, 3.39, and 3.09 points at 25%, 50%, and 75% pruning, and improves DeepSeek-V2-Lite by about 0.79, 4.67, and 8.47 points. On routers trained with renormalization, the direction reverses in the relevant settings: Moonlight gains about 1.68 and 1.60 points at 50% and 75%, and Granite gains about 2.08 points at 50%. The tilted novice adds only 1.2%–1.8% parameters at 50% pruning and improves over the constant novice by 3.45 points on OLMoE and 1.37 points on Moonlight, with Holm-corrected significance. At 75% pruning it improves over the constant on all three main models, with corrected significance on two. On OLMoE, a rank-8 tilted novice beats retaining one additional expert per layer while using about one sixth of the parameters; at 75% pruning it reduces WikiText perplexity from 1434 to 172. Under 4-bit and 3-bit GPTQ, the tilted novice’s gain over the constant is not detectably reduced in 11 of 12 settings, while rerouting gains drop in 11 of 12 settings, significantly in 5.b:T4a9,The authors learn binary gates over attention heads while keeping all model weights frozen. Each head output is multiplied by a hard binary gate in the forward pass; gradients are passed through a sigmoid relaxation with a straight-through estimator. The objective penalizes configurations that exceed a target perplexity ratio while encouraging sparsity. The reported setup initializes all gate logits to 5.0, uses r=1.00, λ=1e-4, AdamW with learning rate 1e-2, batch size 8, and 1,000 optimization steps. The selected pruning mask is the one with the best observed batch-level perplexity ratio during training, not necessarily the final step. Memorization is measured with k-extractability: given the first k tokens, the model is counted as memorizing the sequence if greedy decoding reproduces the next k tokens exactly. The paper evaluates k=16, 32, and 50. For mechanism, it examines attention entropy, softmax saturation, agreement across heads, and the contribution of pruned heads to the residual stream. The key observation is that memorized sequences induce sharper attention and stronger cross-head agreement; pruning task-redundant heads weakens this coordinated retrieval signal.c:T4a1,The study uses three instruction-tuned models: Qwen2.5-7B, Qwen3.5-4B, and Llama-3.1-8B. Qwen3.5 includes many gated-delta linear attention layers. The authors train LoRA adapters for 12 tasks spanning response conventions, behavioral dispositions, and task capabilities: German-only responses, lowercase responses, blanket refusal, chain-of-thought behavior, several misalignment and sycophancy settings, JSON extraction, semantic parsing, and Georgian/Hungarian grapheme-to-phoneme conversion. Each task uses 43–150 evaluation prompts and 20 completions per prompt, so each reported rate aggregates 860–3,000 completions, with 95% confidence intervals bootstrapped over prompts. The main finding is that some fine-tuned behaviors, including German responses and emergent misalignment, can be transferred from the fine-tuned model to the base model through KV-cache patching. Conversely, base→ft patching can partially or fully suppress fine-tuned behavior. Increasing source-cache ownership from prompt-1 to prompt to all generally preserves or strengthens the effect. The token sweep shows that a small number of pivotal positions often carry much of the distributional shift.d:T44d,The evaluation uses τ-bench, AppWorld, and OfficeBench, covering customer-service tool interaction, multi-application API workflows, and office automation. Baselines include SIMIA, an in-context single-shot simulation method, and WM-Training for Qwen3.5-35B-A3B. AWM substantially improves over SIMIA across backbones. With DeepSeek-V4-Pro, AWM @16 reaches 86.00 on τ-bench versus SIMIA’s 60.00, 90.70 on AppWorld versus 37.98, and 77.42/69.47/23.21 on OfficeBench 1App/2Apps/3Apps. With GPT-5.4, AWM @16 reports 82.00 on τ-bench, 87.60 on AppWorld, and 63.44 on OfficeBench 1App, compared with SIMIA’s 50.00, 31.01, and 27.96. With Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, AWM outperforms SIMIA and often matches or exceeds WM-Training; for OfficeBench 3Apps @16, it reports 41.96 versus WM-Training’s 33.04. Removing the Python program tool consistently lowers success rates, with average drops of roughly 5.3–8.1 points for GPT-5.4 and 4.3–7.3 points for Qwen3.5-35B-A3B. Increasing simulator-side compute from M=1 to M=4 yields gains ranging from about 1.2 to 24.1 points depending on benchmark and backbone.e:T590,The authors instantiate continuous diffusion language modeling with LangFlow and speculative drafting with DSpark. DSpark’s Markov and recurrent-head variants are adapted into standalone generators: they no longer receive target-model features and are not verified or corrected by a target LM. The task is unconditional single-block OpenWebText generation at block lengths 16, 128, and 1024. Because there is no semantic prompt or prior block, each model must choose content and maintain the dependencies induced by that choice. LangFlow repeatedly refines continuous representations for the whole sequence, allowing positions to exchange information before tokens are finalized. The DSpark-style models compute backbone features once and rely on a lightweight output head: the Markov head conditions only on the previous sampled token, while the recurrent head carries a hidden state over the generated history. The analysis identifies a concrete limitation of the Markov interface: information about an earlier choice may no longer be recoverable from the immediately preceding token, restricting the class of distributions the head can represent. Recurrent memory removes that particular access bottleneck, although learning to use the memory remains empirical. The paper also separates improved supervision from improved sampling compute by testing teacher probability matching and additional LangFlow denoising steps.f:T57e,The starting point is a continuous flow language model over categorical sequences. Tokens are represented as one-hot vectors, the source distribution is Gaussian noise, and the model learns a denoiser along a continuous path from noise to data. Existing flow-map language models learn the solution operator X_{s,t}, often parameterized through a two-time denoiser δ_{s,t}, so that generation can jump directly from s=0 to t=1 in one forward pass. In the synchronous version, all token positions share the same scalar time, so every position denoises at the same rate.

Asynchronous FMLMs replace this scalar time with tokenwise times τ_i. Each τ_i measures the denoising progress of position i. A scheduler controls how these tokenwise times evolve. The schedule may be prescribed, or it may be learned and state-adaptive, in the spirit of masked diffusion samplers that decide which positions should be resolved earlier. The technical step that makes this well-defined is to treat the tokenwise times as part of the dynamical state. The model then follows a coupled flow over token representations and their time variables. The paper shows that this coupled process defines a valid flow map and derives distillation objectives for learning it. The resulting one-step model predicts both token evolution and time evolution, internalizing an ordering that would otherwise require multiple sampling steps.3:["$","main",null,{"className":"flex-1 overflow-y-auto","children":["$","div",null,{"className":"mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 xl:max-w-6xl","children":["$","$L9",null,{"data":{"date":"2026-11-03","generated_at":"2026-11-04T01:01:49.868784+00:00","stats":{"total":158,"starred":3,"must_read":9,"skim":61,"glance":88,"by_category":{"efficient":19,"eval":21,"mechanism":26,"pretraining_data":2,"synthetic_data":9,"llm_pretrain":4,"scaling":4,"architecture":11,"multimodal":12,"multimodal_gen":9,"post_train":16,"coding_agent":4,"agent":21},"fetch_status":{"arxiv":"ok","huggingface":"ok","blog":"failed","twitter":"unconfigured","github_trending":"empty","hf_trending":"empty"},"fetch_errors":{"blog":"https://qwenlm.github.io/feed.xml: [blog] Client error '404 Not Found' for url 'https://qwenlm.github.io/feed.xml'\nFor more information check: https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Status/404; LLM agent 场景下 speculative decoding 的诊断和训练分配问题。agent 一次任务包含多次模型调用，历史不断变长，调用类型也在变化；单一的平均 acceptance rate 会把浏览、代码修改、工具反馈、最终回答等不同决策混在一起，难以指导 draft model 应该在哪里被蒸馏。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是核心贡献，但对 serving、agent 工作负载建模和小模型 draft 训练有实用启发。关键点是把 speculative decoding 从“静态 prompt 上的 token 级加速问题”，推进到“轨迹中不同决策状态的分布匹配问题”。这对训练小 draft model、构造 agent 推理数据、理解长上下文下的吞吐瓶颈都有参考价值。尤其是论文指出，aggregate acceptance 可能随 context length 上升，但在 matched late browsing actions 内部反而下降，说明只看长度分桶会产生误判。","technical_approach":"TraceSpec 包含两部分。第一部分是评测框架：通过 dynamic execution 在 BrowseComp-Plus 和 SWE-bench Verified 上收集真实 agent 执行中的模型调用，再用 fixed-prefix replay 在相同历史前缀上比较不同 speculative baselines。这样可以把 acceptance、accepted length、throughput 等指标绑定到 workload、execution phase、task progress 和 context strata。第二部分是 draft 训练方法：使用 target-generated trajectories 中的中间 assistant turns 作为监督单元，不只训练最终答案。训练预算有限时，TraceSpec 按 draft-target disagreement 动态分配样本曝光。它用 state-conditioned estimates 区分不同轨迹状态，用 prefix products 估计早期不一致对连续 token 接受的影响，再用 event-normalized sampling 把监督份额转成训练访问次数，避免长 turn 或高 token 数事件支配训练。模型训练仍保留 DSpark 架构和蒸馏目标，主要变化在监督范围和采样分配。","experiments":"数据资产 TraceSpec-Traj 包含 10,000 个训练状态和 2,500 个测试状态，来源任务互不重合，覆盖 browsing 和 software engineering 两类 agent workload。论文比较了 5 个 speculative baselines，覆盖不同 target sizes 和 sampling temperatures，总计 108 个配置。分析发现，在线执行中 aggregate acceptance 可随 context length 增加而上升，但吞吐在最短和最长上下文之间下降；在匹配的 late browsing actions 中，acceptance 反而更低。Replay 进一步显示，不同 workload 的 context trend 可以相反，不同 execution phase 也有不同 acceptance profile。训练结果方面，TraceSpec 在 24 个 replay phase single task consists of many model calls over evolving histories rather than one static prompt. Aggregate acceptance rate hides which decisions are easy or hard for the draft model.","importance":"The contribution is most relevant to inference research and draft-model training, with indirect value for pretraining researchers interested in workload-aware data construction. It reframes speculative decoding for agents as a trajectory-conditioned distribution matching problem. The important finding is that context length alone is an unreliable explanation: aggregate acceptance can increase with length, while matched late-stage browsing actions show lower acceptance. This matters when deciding where to spend a limited distillation budget for a draft model.","technical_approach":"TraceSpec combines dynamic execution and fixed-prefix replay. Dynamic execution records the actual model calls encountered by agents on BrowseComp-Plus and SWE-bench Verified. Fixed-prefix replay then evaluates different drafters on the same saved histories, making acceptance comparable across workload, phase, progress, and context strata. The training component uses intermediate assistant turns from target-generated trajectories as supervision units, rather than only final responses. Under a fixed prediction budget, it prioritizes states based on draft-target disagreement. State-conditioned estimates separate different trajectory conditions; prefix products model how early disagreement limits consecutive acceptance; event-normalized sampling converts supervision shares into training visits while accounting for unequal prediction counts. The underlying DSpark architecture and distillation loss are kept, so the main intervention is in supervision scope and allocation.","experiments":"TraceSpec-Traj contains 10,000 training states and 2,500 test states from disjoint source tasks, spanning browsing and software engineering. In matched late browsing actions, acceptance declines, demonstrating that aggregate context trends can be misleading. Replay results show opposing context trends across workloads and distinct phase-level acceptance profiles. In training experiments, TraceSpec leads all 24 replay phase comparisons and improves generation throughput over DSpark in 15 of 18 settings. needed several steps later.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model research is mainly about representation and cache design rather than pretraining recipes. The key idea is to move compression inside the acting model and use the model’s own intermediate state to decide what has already been absorbed. This is useful for thinking about hybrid attention-recurrent architectures, long agent trajectories, and reusable compact caches. The main caveat is architectural: the method is built around models with native recurrent dynamics such as Gated Delta Networks, so its behavior on standard Transformer-only models remains an open question.","technical_approach":"ASTC is a training-free in-model compression method. Instead of training an external compressor or rewriting history into shorter text, it lets early computation read the full context and then uses recurrent updates to test whether a token’s contribution can be recovered from the retained state. Compressible spans are carried forward as agentic soft tokens in the model’s continuous representation space. Exact values such as file paths, identifiers, API arguments, numbers, and checksums are preserved as hard tokens. This design targets two failure modes of prior compression: an external compressor may preserve information that does not match the acting LLM’s internal task representation, and hard-token summaries can lose information that is not easily serialized into short text.","experiments":"The reported experiments cover long-horizon agent tasks, additional long-context settings, and code-repair tasks. Under full-history compression, it reduces Decode KV interactions by 24.52% with accuracy close to the uncompressed baseline. Ablations show that removing exact-value protection causes sharp accuracy loss, indicating that the hard-token preservation path is central to the method. 系统、router、verifier、reward model、decision LM 的低延迟部署有参考价值。它也提醒预训练研究者：模型架构中的 recurrent / hybrid layer 会改变 serving 约束，KV cache 之外还要处理可复用状态的精确分叉。","technical_approach":"Flide decision models 上，Flide 最高达到约 9.5 倍加速；当一个共享状态对应多个问题时，在不同并发水平下吞吐都领先。精度方面，multi-item scoring 通过把多个问题拼进同一序列获得吞吐，但 delimiter token 会进入上下文并改变 hidden states，导致输出概率偏移；Flide 在 total variation distance 上最接近 inference mismatch for decision models: many short categorical questions share a long context, but current generation-oriented engines either recompute the shared prefix or alter the output probabilities.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it is relevant to foundation-model infrastructure. Decision models, routers, verifiers, reward models, and agent controllers often behave less like text generators and more like confidence-sensitive classifiers over a shared state. Flide shows that serving these models efficiently requires representing request structure explicitly. The recurrent-state part is especially useful for hybrid architectures, where KV-cache reuse alone is not enough.","technical_approach":"Flide separates the logical request tree from the physical token layout. Logically, a request has a shared trunk and multiple branches; each branch must attend only to the trunk and itself. Physically, Flide flattens a batch of such trees into one GPU token stream and stores dependency metadata on device. ForkAttention uses this metadata to enforce branch isolation for standard attention. For Gated DeltaNet-style recurrent layers, it forks the recurrent state from the trunk so that each branch starts from the exact shared state instead of inheriting sibling-branch information. The system also supports graph reuse for different request compositions, checkpointing for growing agent histories, and an optional batch-invariant mode that fixes floating-point reduction order.","experiments":"The evaluation compares Flide with vLLM, SGLang, and multi-item scoring workloads ranging from one question per passage to long articles and agent trajectories with many questions. The paper reports up to 9.5x faster answering on public hybrid models and dedicated decision models. Throughput is higher across concurrency levels whenever multiple questions share a state. On output fidelity, Flide stays closest to float32 reference distributions under total variation distance, while multi-item scoring gains throughput by inserting delimiter tokens that shift hidden states and can flip decisions.","reading_guide":"Skim time: about 20–30 minutes. what should the layer compute when the router still selects that expert. Prior pruning methods often change both the expert mask and the replacement behavior at the same time, making it hard to tell whether gains come from better expert selection or from a better replacement operator.","importance":"The main lesson for MoE pretraining and deployment is that routing semantics are part of the trained model, not a minor implementation detail. Whether the router was trained with top-k gate renormalization determines whether pruned models should preserve the original gate scale or renormalize the surviving gates. The paper reports differences of roughly 0.8 to 8.5 points from this choice alone, sometimes larger than the effect of the expert-selection criterion. This matters for MoE model design, compression recipes, and checkpoint formats: a pruned checkpoint may need to retain router rows for removed experts if the correct trained-scale behavior is to be reproduced.","technical_approach":"The paper isolates replacement from selection by holding the pruning mask fixed and varying only the computation used for removed expert slots. It compares mean-output constants, low-rank input-dependent maps, survivor-based replacements, equal-budget compressed copies, and two rerouting variants that differ only in gate scaling. The first key contribution is the trained-scale rule: use the same gate-scale convention at pruning time that the router saw during training. For routers trained without top-k renormalization, keep the original survivor gate scale; for routers trained with renormalization, use the renormalized scale. The second contribution is the tilted novice, a low-rank linear map from the pruned expert’s input, fitted in closed form from streamed calibration statistics. Its rank-0 case is the MoNE-style constant novice, while higher ranks recover limited input dependence at a small parameter cost and without gradient-based retraining.","experiments":"$a","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. extrapolation 的问题从 AR LM 扩展到 continuous diffusion LM，并指出 diffusion LM 的长文本退化不只是位置编码越界，还包括 conditioning attention 改变和远程上下文缺失。对基座研究的启发是：非 AR 语言模型的推理结构会改变长上下文问题的形态，不能简单套用 AR 指标更值得看。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets length extrapolation for continuous diffusion language models. When these models generate sequences longer than those seen in training, they can suffer from repetition, semantic drift, and degraded generation quality. Methods designed for autoregressive LMs, such as RoPE scaling or sliding-window attention, do not directly address the bidirectional, whole-sequence denoising process used by diffusion LMs.","importance":"This is relevant to alternative language-modeling paradigms. The main contribution is not a new pretraining objective, but a clearer treatment of long-context inference for continuous diffusion LMs. The paper identifies three failure sources: positional mismatch beyond the training range, unstable attention to conditioning tokens, and loss of distant context under local attention. This is a useful distinction because it shows why AR context-extension recipes cannot be transferred mechanically to diffusion-based text generators.","technical_approach":"RangeSWA is training-free and leaves model weights unchanged. For each body-token query, it builds a unified attention candidate set containing control tokens, a centered local window, dynamically retrieved exact remote tokens, and remote block summaries. These candidates are combined under one softmax, allowing local details, conditioning information, and selected long-range information to compete within the same attention readout. The method also adds positional remapping to reduce out-of-range positional distances, conditioning attention correction to compensate for attention redistribution caused by windowing, and denoising-stage-adaptive window scheduling to vary the attention span across denoising stages. Compared with standard sliding-window attention, it keeps the stabilizing effect of local attention while adding controlled access to distant content.","experiments":"The paper evaluates extrapolation from 1k to 8k tokens on ELF-B, ELF-M, and ELF-L, and also checks applicability to CoLa and LangFlow. Compared with standard sliding-window attention, RangeSWA reduces generative perplexity and repetition rates on ELF-M/L. The ablations indicate that conditioning attention correction and adaptive window scheduling explain most of the perplexity gains, while remote context access is more important for reducing repetition. The paper also notes that generative perplexity alone can hide long-form degeneration, so repetition metrics are needed. The provided content does not include the full numeric tables, so the safest reading is qualitative rather than number-driven.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. Many NIAH-style benchmarks rely on synthetic data, reused public datasets, or manual annotation, which creates distribution mismatch, contamination risk, and maintenance cost. More importantly, many multi-hop variants still reduce reasoning to a single fixed chain, while real long-context use often requires search over multiple possible proof paths.","importance":"This is an evaluation contribution rather than a pretraining method. Its value for foundation-model research is that it stresses a capability that long-context models often claim but do not always demonstrate: combining retrieval with structured search over a large context. It is relevant for long-context pretraining, context-extension recipes, and evaluation design, because it separates simple fact lookup from graph-like reasoning under distractors.","technical_approach":"The benchmark is built from real scientific papers and citation links. A pipeline samples subgraphs from Semantic Scholar, where nodes are papers and edges are cites or cited_by relations. To reduce contamination, the sampled papers are chosen after the evaluated model’s training cutoff. The graph is converted into a long-context QA instance by including paper information and bibliographies in the prompt, while the answer is computed directly from graph reachability. The benchmark defines YES/NO reachability, multiple-choice reachability, and reachability counting. It exposes control knobs such as path depth, subgraph size, target context length, and distractor type. The main design difference from standard NIAH is that the answer depends on graph reachability rather than retrieving a single hidden fact or following a deterministic chain.","experiments":"The paper evaluates eight models: GPT-5.6-Sol, GPT-5.6-Luna, Gemini-3.1-Pro, Gemini-3.8-Flash, Gemini-3.6-Flash, and three Qwen3.5 variants: 397B-A17B, 122B-A10B, and 35B-A3B. The provided content clearly reports one main trend: accuracy drops as question depth increases. At large depths, GPT-5.6 and Qwen3.5 models perform no better than random guessing on YES/NO questions, often by over-answering NO. This suggests that long context length alone does not imply robust multi-hop graph search, especially when the prompt contains disconnected components and irrelevant citation evidence.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. 评测有参考价值。它把恢复能力从一个工程启发式组件，拆成可测量的行为能力：恢复到某个视觉上相似的界面并不够，关键是从该状态继续执行同一后续动作时，能否得到一致结果。这个定义比只看截图相似度更接近真实环境状态，尤其适合桌面、文件系统、办公软件这类存在隐藏状态和副作用的场景。","technical_approach":"核心框架是 ReTrace。论文先把 computer-use task 写成 POMDP，强调 agent 只能看到 observation，但真实状态还包含隐藏环境变量。为避免只评估表面视觉恢复，作者提出 Transition-Consistent Recovery：如果从恢复状态和目标状态出发，执行同一段后续动作后得到相同任务结果，就认为恢复成功。实现上，ReTrace 会先收集 on-policy 成功轨迹，并多次 replay 同一轨迹，只保留结果稳定的轨迹，以减少环境随机性。随后从成功轨迹中采样中间状态作为恢复目标，再构造扰动后的错误状态，测试 agent 能否通过模型行为恢复到目标附近，并继续完成剩余任务。这个设计的重点不是让 agent 重新做整题，而是隔离“恢复”这一能力。","experiments":"实验在 OSWorld 和 WindowsAgentArena 上运行，覆盖闭源和开源 computer-use agents。文中给出的主要结论有三点。第一，oracle recovery 相比 naive environment reset 能显著提高任务完成率，说明很多失败并非模型完全不会做任务，而是中途错误破坏了后续状态。第二，现有 agent 自发恢复的倾向和能力都较弱；它们通常继续执行、重试或偏离任务，而不是有目标地撤销副作用。第三，在加入显式 model-based recovery 后，恢复成功率提升超过一个数量级；但恢复质量不稳定时也会伤害端到端完成率，因为错误恢复会把轨迹带到更难继续的状态。已给出的全文片段没有包含具体表格数值，因此不宜补数字。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 20–30 分钟。重点看 Introduction 中对三类恢复方案的限制分析，以及 Preliminary/Method 中 过滤非确定轨迹、用目标导向后续动作验证隐藏状态。这部分比具体榜单数值更有复用价值。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies whether computer-use agents can recover from mid-trajectory mistakes in long-horizon desktop tasks without relying on full environment resets, API-level undo, or replaying the entire action history.","importance":"The work is more relevant to general agent evaluation than to foundation-model pretraining. Its useful contribution is a behavioral definition of recovery. A recovered state is not judged by visual similarity alone; it is judged by whether the same downstream actions produce the same outcome as from the target state. This matters for desktop environments where hidden state, files, application buffers, and side effects often determine whether continuation is possible.","technical_approach":"The proposed framework, ReTrace, formalizes computer-use tasks as POMDPs and defines successful recovery through transition consistency. The pipeline first collects successful on-policy trajectories, replays them multiple times, and keeps only trajectories with deterministic successful outcomes. It then samples intermediate states as recovery targets, perturbs them into erroneous states, and evaluates whether an agent can restore a state from which replaying the remaining actions still succeeds. This isolates recovery from general task-solving ability and avoids over-crediting agents that merely return to a visually similar screen.","experiments":"The evaluation is instantiated on OSWorld and WindowsAgentArena with both closed- and open-source agents. The reported findings are qualitative but clear from the available text: oracle recovery improves performance over naive resets, indicating that many failures hide recoverable task-solving capacity; current agents rarely recover autonomously under erroneous conditions; explicit model-based recovery raises recovery success by more than an order of magnitude; and recovery quality directly affects end-to-end task completion, with weak recovery sometimes derailing the trajectory. c","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"mechanism","research_focus":{"version":1,"tracks":[],"training_stages":["unknown"],"stage_basis":"unspecified","stage_evidence":[],"synthetic_methods":[]},"cover_url":"/covers/2610_40974.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"arxiv_url":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40974","source_url":null,"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文问的是：部署后的语言模型能否在不重训、不知道具体泄露样本的情况下，通过剪掉冗余 或下游指标保持不变，并不代表模型的记忆行为保持不变。","technical_approach":"方法很直接。作者在冻结模型参数的前提下，为每个 attention head 学一个二值 gate。前向使用 hard gate，反向用 straight-through estimator 传梯度。优化目标在限制 perplexity 不上升的同时鼓励更多 gate 关闭。具体设置包括：gate logit 初始化为 5.0，使初始状态接近全开；允许 perplexity ratio r=1.00；稀疏正则系数 λ=1e-4；AdamW 学习率 1e-2；batch size 8；优化 1000 steps。最终不取最后一步，而是选择训练过程中 batch-level perplexity ratio 最低的 gate 配置，并在推理时把对应 head 输出置零。记忆定义采用 k-extractability：给定前 k 个 token，如果 greedy decoding 逐字复现后 k 个 token，则认为该序列被记忆。作者使用 k=16、32、50 三档，覆盖短跨度和既有抽取研究中的常用阈值。机制分析部分关注四类诊断信号，其中核心是注意力熵、softmax saturation、跨 head agreement，以及被剪 head 对 residual stream 中记忆方向的贡献。结论是，记忆样本会诱发更尖锐的注意力分布和更强的多头一致性；剪掉对任务冗余的 head 会削弱这种集体信号。","experiments":"实验模型包括 Pythia-2.8B-Deduped、Pythia-6.9B-Deduped 和 GPT-2-XL 1.5B。Pythia 的记忆样本来自公开的 memorized sequences 列表，每个模型采样 20,000 条；GPT-2-XL 则在 OpenWebText 上按同一 k-extractability 标准构造，得到约 4,800 到 5,590 条唯一记忆样本，数量随 k 变化。每个模型还构造等量 matched non-memorized set。性能保持用 WikiText-103 和 LAMBADA 评估，包括 perplexity，以及 LAMBADA last-word accuracy；gate 优化使用与最终评测不重合的 held-out split。论文报告的主结果是：在保持用于寻找冗余 head 的语言建模指标基本不受损的条件下，推理时剪掉相当比例的 task-redundant heads，可以显著减少被逐字复现的训练序列。消融和诊断结果支持一个机制解释：记忆序列相比非记忆序列更容易触发低熵注意力和多头对齐；剪枝减少了这些 head 对 residual stream 的叠加贡献，因此降低 greedy 解码下的 verbatim continuation 概率。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 20 到 30 分钟。重点看 Methodology 中 gate 学习目标、k-extractability 定义、记忆/非记忆样本构造方式，以及机制诊断指标。实验部分应优先看三类图表：剪枝比例与记忆复现数量的关系；WikiText-103/LAMBADA 性能是否稳定；记忆样本与非记忆样本在 attention entropy、cross-head agreement 上的差异。需要带着一个限制去读：该方法是 inference-time 干预，效果依赖 greedy decoding、模型族和被选用的任务保持指标；它不能替代训练前数据治理，也不能保证对 sampling、长文本抽取或不同语料分布同样有效。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies whether verbatim memorization can be reduced after deployment by pruning redundant attention heads, without retraining the model or identifying the specific memorized records in advance.","importance":"The main value is mechanistic rather than algorithmic for pretraining. It links memorization to inference-time attention computation: some verbatim recall appears to rely on coordinated, low-entropy attention heads, not only on static storage in MLP weights or parameters in general. This matters for privacy analysis, deduplication policy, model compression, and safety evaluation. It also shows that preserving perplexity or task accuracy does not imply preserving the model’s memorization profile.","technical_approach":"$b","experiments":"The experiments use Pythia-2.8B-Deduped, Pythia-6.9B-Deduped, and GPT-2-XL 1.5B. For Pythia, the authors sample 20,000 memorized sequences per model from the public memorization list. For GPT-2-XL, they construct an equivalent set from OpenWebText using the same extractability criterion, yielding roughly 4,800 to 5,590 unique memorized sequences depending on k. Each model is paired with an equal-sized matched non-memorized set. Language modeling quality is evaluated on WikiText-103 and LAMBADA, using perplexity and LAMBADA last-word accuracy; the gate optimization split is held out from final evaluation. The reported result is that pruning a substantial fraction of task-redundant heads substantially reduces verbatim reproduction while preserving the benchmark performance used to identify redundant heads. The diagnostic results support the interpretation that memorized examples produce a collective attention signal, and head pruning reduces part of that signal before it is accumulated in the residual stream.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. answer token 时的即时计算里，还是已经写入了 prompt 和已生成 token 的 KV cache 表示中，并通过 attention 影响后续输出。","importance":"它对 pretrain ↔ posttrain 接口有启发。微调不只是改变某个输出层决策，也可能改变模型对上下文 token 的编码方式；这些改变会被 KV cache 保存，并在后续生成中持续发挥作用。这个观察会影响对 LoRA 微调、行为安全、cache 复用、adapter 切换和推理时干预的理解。它不是预训练算法论文，但提供了一个清楚的机制分析工具。","technical_approach":"方法是双向 KV-cache patching。作者先用 LoRA 把 base model 微调出不同类型的行为，然后把 fine-tuned model 的 KV cache 注入 base model，记为 ft→base；也反过来把 base cache 注入 fine-tuned model，记为 base→ft。patch 范围分三档：prompt-1 覆盖除最后一个 prompt token 外的 prompt cache；prompt 覆盖完整 prompt cache；all 覆盖 prompt 和生成 token 的 cache。除此之外，作者还做 token-by-token sweep：在 teacher-forced completion 上逐步增加被替换的 cache 长度，用 KL-based proximity 衡量目标模型的 next-token distribution 更接近 base 还是 fine-tune。这个设计能区分行为变化来自早期上下文处理，还是主要来自生成阶段参数本身。","experiments":"实验覆盖 3 个 instruction-tuned 模型：Qwen2.5-7B、Qwen3.5-4B 和 Llama-3.1-8B。其中 Qwen3.5 含有多层 gated-delta linear attention。作者为 12 类任务训练 LoRA，包括固定回答风格，例如德语回答、小写回答、拒答、强制 CoT；行为倾向，例如 emergent misalignment、sycophancy；以及能力类任务，例如 JSON 抽取、语义解析、Georgian/Hungarian G2P。每个任务有 43–150 个评测 prompt，每个 prompt 采样 20 个 completion，因此每个报告率聚合 860–3000 个 completion，并用按 prompt bootstrap 的 95% 置信区间。主要结果是：某些行为可以通过 ft→base cache patching 转移到 base model，例如德语回答和 emergent misalignment；base→ft patching 也能部分或完全移除微调行为。随着 source cache 覆盖范围从 prompt-1 到 prompt 再到 all 增加，行为转移或抑制通常保持稳定或增强。token sweep 显示，少数关键位置承担了大部分分布迁移。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 20–30 分钟。先看 Method，尤其是 prompt-1、prompt、all 三种 patch 设置，否则结果图不容易读。然后看 Figure 1 的双向转移结果，关注哪些行为能被 cache 迁移，哪些不能。token-by-token sweep 和 KL proximity 的部分最有机制分析价值，可以帮助判断关键行为信息集中在哪些上下文位置。附录里的任务定义、评分规则、coherence 检查和 repetition loop 检查适合按需查看。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks where fine-tuned behavior is instantiated during inference: in the computations that select answer tokens, or already in the KV-cache representations of the prompt and previous tokens that later attention reads from.","importance":"The result is useful for understanding the interface between pretraining, fine-tuning, and inference-time state. Fine-tuning can change how a model encodes the preceding context, and those changes can persist in the KV cache and steer later generation. This matters for LoRA analysis, safety-related fine-tunes, cache reuse across adapters, and inference-time interventions. The contribution is mainly mechanistic rather than a new training method.","technical_approach":"The paper introduces bidirectional KV-cache patching. A base model is LoRA fine-tuned to induce specific behaviors. The authors then insert the fine-tuned model’s KV cache into the base model, denoted ft→base, and also insert the base model’s cache into the fine-tuned model, denoted base→ft. They test three cache-coverage levels: prompt-1, which patches all prompt tokens except the last one; prompt, which patches the full prompt cache; and all, which also patches generated-token caches. They further run a token-by-token sweep under teacher forcing and use a KL-based proximity score to measure whether the target model’s next-token distribution is closer to the base or the fine-tuned model. This setup separates behavior mediated by context representations from behavior that only appears during answer-token generation.","experiments":"$c","reading_guide":"A productive skim takes about 20–30 minutes. The token-by-token sweep and KL-proximity analysis are the most useful parts for mechanistic interpretation, since they locate where the cache begins to carry the fine-tuned behavior. code execution. The key issue is not tool access alone, but learning when a GUI action is the right abstraction and when a command or library call is more reliable.","importance":"This is primarily an agent post-training and synthetic-environment paper rather than a foundation-model pretraining paper. Its useful lesson for pretraining-oriented work is about data generation. Teacher quality is mediated by the harness: tool schemas, prompts, context handling, and execution environments change the demonstrations a student receives. The workflow-first task generator is also relevant for building large-scale agent corpora without paying the full construction cost per task.","technical_approach":"Mage-VL-Cowork is a 4B multimodal computer-use agent initialized from a general multimodally aligned checkpoint. It consumes desktop screenshots and emits coordinate-level mouse and keyboard actions, while also being able to execute terminal commands and code in the same episode. Training follows a five-stage curriculum: GUI-oriented midtraining, GUI agent SFT, coding and terminal SFT, hybrid CUA SFT, and online hybrid RL. The data pipeline, Mage-CUA-Forge, builds a skill taxonomy and reusable generation workflows. Application-specific preparers materialize concrete desktop states, such as spreadsheets or mail profiles, and each generated task includes an instruction, setup script, and evaluation rubric. A central design choice is harness evolution: the authors tune and prune tool interfaces, prompts, scaffolds, and shell environments before large-scale teacher rollout, and instantiate separate GUI, terminal, and hybrid harnesses for different training stages.","experiments":"The training corpus contains 293,806 synthesized desktop and web tasks, 6,915 atomic skills, and 318,837 teacher trajectories. buffer，从而减少显存，同时不损失训练效果。","importance":"对预训练和后训练接口有直接启发。它不是新的蒸馏 loss，而是把已有的 teacher 轨迹当作优化器状态来复用，属于训练系统和优化器状态设计的结合点。贡献量级主要在 memory-efficient training：在已有 EMA teacher 的场景下，隐藏层矩阵不再需要单独的 Muon momentum buffer。这个思路对 continual pretrain、self-distillation、on-policy training 都有参考意义，因为这些场景经常已经维护一个随 student 更新的 teacher 或 reference model。","technical_approach":"核心观察是 teacher-student displacement 不是普通的参数差，而是一个对历史更新的指数加权记录。若 teacher 按 EMA 跟随 student，student 每一步更新后，二者差值会累计过去应用到 student 上的 update，并随 EMA 系数衰减。在线性局部模型下，它还能平滑时间上不相关的噪声。Muon-RT 的实现没有直接把这个位移当 momentum 使用，因为 Muon 的 update 会经过 Newton-Schulz 正交化，直接混入可能让奇异方向被不受控放大。作者采用更保守的设计：先对当前 gradient 做无 momentum 的 Muon 正交化；再计算 teacher-student displacement；去掉与 student weight 平行的径向分量；用最大奇异值估计做谱归一化；最后在正交化之后用小固定系数把历史方向与当前方向相加。这样保留 Muon 的几何约束，也避免把 teacher 位移放进 Newton-Schulz 迭代里放大。方法只适用于 student 与 EMA teacher 参数一一对齐的场景，不适用于独立 teacher。","experiments":"实验覆盖 SDFT 和 SDPO 两类自蒸馏设置。文中给出的 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 持久状态显存估算显示，AdamW 约 129.0GB，带 momentum 的 Muon 约 103.2GB，Muon-RT 约 75.4GB。相对 AdamW，Muon-RT 少 42% 持久显存；相对 momentum Muon，少 27%。效果上，SDFT 中 Muon-RT 平均准确率比 AdamW 高 2.6 个点，比 momentum Muon 高 5.4 个点；SDPO 中比 AdamW 高 2.0 个点。作者还比较了多个 Muon 变体，Muon-RT 在两个 host 的六组设置里总体排名最好。理论部分给出 teacher-student displacement 与历史 applied updates 的关系，并在正则性、目标漂移有界、方向对齐等假设下证明期望 objective gap 线性收缩到残差水平。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 Introduction 和方法部分，重点看 teacher-student displacement 的推导、Muon-RT 的 update 顺序，以及 Figure 1 的显存和准确率对比。理论证明可以选择性看 Lemma 1 和 Lemma 2：它们解释为什么 EMA teacher 的参数差可以提供 momentum-like 信号。实现时最值得注意的是三个细节：去掉径向分量、谱归一化、正交化之后再混合。预计阅读 25 到 35 self-distillation for LLMs. In SDFT and SDPO, training already keeps both student and EMA teacher weights. AdamW or momentum Muon adds extra optimizer states. The question is whether the teacher trajectory itself can replace Muon’s momentum buffer by using the teacher-student displacement.","importance":"The main value is in optimizer-state reuse for self-distillation and related post-training or continual-training pipelines. The method does not introduce a new distillation objective. It reinterprets the EMA teacher as a source of optimization history. This is useful for settings where an aligned EMA teacher or mean teacher is already maintained, because the memory reduction comes almost for free at the algorithmic level.","technical_approach":"The key observation is that the teacher-student displacement stores an exponentially weighted history of updates applied to the student. Under gradient descent it is proportional to gradient momentum; under Muon it tracks past orthogonalized update directions. Muon-RT does not feed this displacement directly into Newton-Schulz orthogonalization. Instead, it first computes the current gradient direction with momentum-free Muon, separately forms the teacher-student displacement, removes the radial component parallel to the student weight matrix, normalizes the remaining direction by an estimate of its spectral norm, and mixes it with the orthogonalized current update using a small fixed coefficient. This design keeps the current gradient path controlled and uses the teacher displacement only as a post-orthogonalization historical direction.","experiments":"The experiments cover SDFT and SDPO. For Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, the reported persistent-state memory is 129.0GB for AdamW, 103.2GB for momentum Muon, and 75.4GB for Muon-RT. That is a 42% reduction relative to AdamW and 27% relative to momentum Muon. In SDFT, Muon-RT improves average accuracy by 2.6 points over AdamW and 5.4 points over momentum Muon. In SDPO, it improves by 2.0 points over AdamW. The paper also compares Muon variants and reports that Muon-RT ranks first across six settings from two host models. The theory shows that the displacement is a discounted history of applied updates and establishes linear contraction to a residual error level under regularity, bounded drift, and alignment assumptions.","reading_guide":"Skim the introduction for the memory accounting and the motivation, then read the method section carefully to understand the update order. speculative decoding 中的 target model 验证和纠错，让 drafter 架构自己作为独立生成模型工作，它和 continuous diffusion language model 在建模文本分布时差在哪里。通常 speculative drafter 被评估的是加速目标模型的能力，而 CDLM 被评估的是独立生成质量，这两个设置不对称。论文把二者放到同一个 standalone generation 条件下比较。","importance":"这篇论文对新型语言建模范式有直接价值。它把 speculative drafting 从 serving 技术中剥离出来，考察其架构本身的生成能力，并和连续扩散 LM 的全序列迭代 refinement 做对照。贡献不在于给出更大的模型或更好的 benchmark 数字，而在于澄清两类并行生成方法的能力边界：one-pass drafter 的瓶颈来自生成历史的可访问信息和输出头表达方式；CDLM 的优势来自多轮全序列状态交换，且同一组参数可以通过增加采样步数继续改善生成质量。这对判断非 AR 语言模型、block generation、speculative head 是否能从“加速组件”走向“独立生成器”有参考意义。","technical_approach":"论文将 CDLM 实例化为 LangFlow，将 speculative drafting 实例化为 DSpark，并把 DSpark 的 Markov head 与 recurrent head 改造成无 target feature、无 verifier 的 standalone generator。任务是 OpenWebText 的 unconditional single-block generation，长度分别为 16、128、1024。这里没有语义 prompt，也没有之前 block 的上下文；模型必须自己选择主题、实体和后续依赖。LangFlow 的机制是反复 refinement 整个序列的连续表示，使不同位置在 token 最终确定前可以交换信息。DSpark-style drafter 则更接近一次 backbone pass 后由轻量输出头生成 token：Markov head 只看前一个生成 token，recurrent head 通过循环状态保留更早历史。论文的理论部分刻画了 Markov head 的一个具体限制：早期内容选择的信息可能无法从相邻前 token 中恢复，因此某些跨距离依赖分布无法表示。recurrent memory 可以移除这一特定信息访问障碍，但是否学会有效利用历史仍是经验问题。论文还比较了 teacher next-token probability supervision 和 generation-time compute：前者改变训练目标但不增加历史访问，后者让 LangFlow 在固定权重下通过更多 denoising step 改善样本。","experiments":"实验从零训练各长度设置下的模型，使用相同 OpenWebText 数据、相同 backbone 尺寸和训练 update budget，并取消 target features 与 verification。评估使用两个互补指标：外部语言模型给出的 generation perplexity，也就是 GenPPL；以及 MAUVE，用来衡量生成分布和参考文本分布的相似性。主要结果是：在长度 128 和 1024 下，LangFlow 在 GenPPL 和 MAUVE 上都优于 DSpark-Markov 与 DSpark-RNN；在长度 16 下，DSpark-RNN 更强。recurrent head 在所有测试长度上都优于 Markov head，说明历史记忆确实缓解了相邻 token 接口的信息瓶颈，但长序列上仍未关闭与 LangFlow 的差距。teacher probability matching 能降低 GenPPL，但没有带来可观察的 MAUVE 提升，说明更像外部 evaluator 的 next-token 偏好不等于更接近真实文本分布。固定 LangFlow 权重、增加 denoising steps 后，GenPPL 和 MAUVE We introduce asynchronous flow map language models (aFMLMs), which incorporate prediction order into one-step modeling by learning the flow maps of asynchronous flows. one-step parallel language modeling 中的顺序建模问题。AR 模型通过从左到右的生成步骤使用顺序信息；masked diffusion 模型也能在多轮填 mask 的过程中选择生成顺序。但 one-step flow map LM 只有一次前向传播，所有 token 同时生成，传统意义上的生成顺序没有可执行的时间轴。因此问题变成：如何让一个单步生成模型仍然利用 X_{s,t}，并用 two-time denoiser δ_{s,t} 通过自蒸馏学习任意时间跳转，最终可用一次 s=0 到 t=1 的跳转生成完整序列。问题在于同步 FMLM 使用单一全局时间，所有位置以相同速度 denoise，无法表达哪个 token 先被“确定”。\n\nAsynchronous FMLM 的关键改动是给每个 token 位置分配自己的时间 τ_i。τ_i 表示该位置的 denoising progress，不同 token 可以处在不同生成阶段。调度器控制 τ_i 的演化，可以是预设顺序，也可以是依赖当前状态的 adaptive schedule，后者借鉴 masked diffusion sampler 中根据模型置信度或状态选择下一步的思想。为了让这个构造在数学上仍然是合法 flow，论文把 tokenwise time τ 也纳入系统状态，使生成过程变成 x 和 τ 的耦合流。这样不仅 token 表示在流动，时间调度本身也在流动。随后论文推导 asynchronous flow map，并设计蒸馏目标，让单个模型同时预测 token 表示如何前进、tokenwise time 如何前进。\n\n和 AR 的区别是，它不在输出空间逐 token 展开；和 MDM 的区别是，它不需要多轮外部采样步骤；和同步 FMLM 的区别是，它允许不同位置有不同进度，从而把顺序信息编码进单步生成模型的内部计算。","experiments":"实验验证集中在 one-step generation。论文报告 asynchronous FMLM 在 Sudoku solving 和 GSM8K math problem 的代码生成设置中，取得了被比较方法里最好的单步生成表现。关键对照应包括同步 FMLM、不同预设 Autoregressive models exploit order through left-to-right generation, and masked diffusion models can choose an order across iterative denoising steps. A one-step flow-map model has no external sequence of generation steps: all tokens are produced in a single forward pass. The central question is how such a model can still benefit from a prediction order or schedule.","importance":"This is a modeling-paradigm paper, not a standard AR pretraining recipe paper. Its main contribution is to move ordering information from an external sampling loop into the state space of a continuous flow, then distill that behavior into a one-step flow map. The idea is relevant to diffusion/flow language models, any-order modeling, and low-latency generation. It suggests a route where fast generation is achieved not by speculative decoding or serving tricks, but by changing how the joint distribution is factorized and compressed into hidden computation. The current evidence is strongest on structured generation tasks such as Sudoku and code generation for GSM8K, so it should be read as a strong conceptual and mechanistic result rather than a full-scale replacement for AR language modeling.","technical_approach":"$f","experiments":"The experiments focus on one-step generation. Architecturally, the causal attention mask in every layer is replaced with a bidirectional mask, so prompt, answer, and padding positions can attend to each other. The only trainable parameters are LoRA adapters on self-attention query and value projections, with rank 1024, alpha 1024, and zero dropout. Sparse attention reduces the number of edges in the attention graph, but it does not by itself reduce expert computation. Sparse experts reduce conditional feed-forward execution, but they do not guarantee that document-level information remains connected. This distinction matters for long-context DLM design, where denoiser calls, attention graph structure, expert routing, dispatch cost, and sampling steps must be reported separately. The contribution is closer to a formal and measurement-oriented analysis than to a fully validated new training recipe.","technical_approach":"The paper explicitly separates diffusion time from layer depth inside the denoiser: S sampling updates imply S denoiser evaluations, not a single L-step trajectory through an L-layer network. For attention, it defines allowed key sets using local, dilated, and global neighborhoods, with normalization over the full allowed key set. This avoids treating individual query-key pairs as independently normalized scores. For expert layers, it defines router probabilities, selected expert sets, and an activation budget k, while keeping execution, residency, optimizer state, capacity constraints, token dropping, and dispatch communication as separate implementation quantities. It also stresses that inference samplers must match the prediction convention used in training; clean-state prediction, noise prediction, and velocity prediction are not interchangeable.","experiments":"The paper reports an analysis of a sparse-expert configuration called DualBudget across scientific summarization, multi-hop question answering, dialogue, and sentence-pair prediction, with aggregate scores, length sweeps, ablations, and cost values treated as separate records. 只奖励最终答案正确，不能保证模型真的依赖 CoT 得出答案；已有 CoT causality 指标通过删除步骤或注入错误来扰动 CoT，但这些操作会改变每一步的信息内容，容易把“对信息变化敏感”误当成“推理链有因果作用”。","importance":"这篇工作对预训练研究的直接贡献不大，但对 post-train 训练信号设计有启发。它把 reward 从 outcome correctness 推向 reasoning-process causality，并指出过程奖励如果构造不好，会诱导更短、更空、更少探索的 CoT。这个观察对 RLVR、可验证奖励、推理数据自举都有参考价值：训练信号不仅要可验证，还要尽量避免把错误的行为模式奖励进去。","technical_approach":"CRoS 的核心做法是打乱 CoT 中 reasoning steps 的顺序，而不改写每个 step 的内容。给定原始 CoT 和最终答案，方法比较模型在原始 CoT 条件下生成原答案的概率，以及在 scrambled CoT 条件下生成同一答案的概率。如果打乱顺序后答案概率下降，说明模型更依赖原始推理顺序，CoT 的因果作用更强。这个设计区别于 truncation-based metric 和 error-insertion metric：前者删除信息，后者加入错误信息，都会引入内容层面的混杂；CRoS 主要扰动结构顺序，尽量保留局部信息内容。训练时，CRoS 被作为 outcome reward 之外的辅助 reward，用于强化更有因果作用的推理链。","experiments":"实验使用 Gemma-3-4B-IT，在 Reasoning Gym 中的 AIW（logic）和 hard 难度上略优，分别为 33.5% vs. 33.0%、31.5% vs. 31.0%。CoT 质量上，CRoS 的平均 CoT quality score 为 43.4%，baseline 为 41.4%。Pass@64 中，base model 为 86.9%，outcome reward 为 85.4%，CRoS 为 88.3%。faithfulness 评估里，CRoS 的 hint 的批评，这部分定义了 reward 混杂的来源；再看 CRoS 的 reward 构造和 Figure 1，确认它到底扰动了什么、保留了什么。实验部分建议看 OOD accuracy、CoT-Pass@K、hint acknowledgment 三类结果。需要留意的限制是：scrambling step order 仍然可能改变全局语义连贯性，它测到的是“顺序依赖”与“因果推理使用”的混合信号；此外结果主要在小模型和 Reasoning Gym 上验证，是否能迁移到大规模 RLVR 训练还需要更强证据。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a gap in RLVR: rewarding only final-answer correctness does not ensure that the chain of thought actually drives the answer. Existing CoT causality metrics perturb the rationale by deleting steps or injecting errors, but these interventions also change the information inside the steps, making it hard to separate true causal reliance from sensitivity to altered content.","importance":"The work is more relevant to post-training signal design than to base-model pretraining, but it has useful implications for reasoning training. It shows that process rewards can be harmful when the intervention used to measure process quality is confounded. For RLVR and synthetic reasoning pipelines, the main lesson is that verifiable rewards need to reward the right dependency structure, not just the right answer or a superficially plausible rationale.","technical_approach":"CRoS measures CoT causality by scrambling the order of reasoning steps while preserving the content of each step. Given a CoT and its final answer, it compares the probability of the original answer under the original rationale versus the reordered rationale. A larger probability drop after scrambling is treated as evidence that the original step order causally supports the answer. This differs from truncation-based metrics, which remove information, and error-insertion metrics, which add incorrect information. In training, CRoS is used as an auxiliary reward on top of the standard outcome reward.","experiments":"The experiments train Gemma-3-4B-IT on AIW and Survo from Reasoning Gym, then evaluate out-of-domain generalization on five unseen datasets from the same broad categories. CRoS reaches 46.8% average OOD accuracy, compared with 43.8% for the strongest causality baseline and 41.8% for outcome-only reward. For in-domain generalization, it matches the strongest baselines at the default difficulty with 34.5% accuracy, and is slightly better on harder settings: 33.5% versus 33.0% on medium-hard, and 31.5% versus 31.0% on hard. On CoT quality, CRoS obtains 43.4% average CoT quality versus 41.4% for baselines. With 64 samples, answer accuracy is 88.3% for CRoS, compared with 86.9% for the base model and 85.4% for outcome reward. On faithfulness, the hint acknowledgment score is 34.0%, compared with 20.2% for the strongest baseline.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. supervision，但如果教师信号质量不可控，token-level loss 会把错误偏好更高效地写入模型。TESD 的价值在于把“教师是否可靠”显式建模，并让教师角色也参与优化。这对后续研究有两个提示：一是 dense supervision 不一定优于 sparse reward，关键在信号可信度；二是同一个模型在 student/teacher 复述，而不是生成可独立成立的解法。基于这些奖励，TESD 做两类优化。第一是 teacher-oriented optimization，把教师 rollout 的奖励作为训练目标，提升模型在带 privileged context 条件下产生可靠指导的能力。第二是 adaptive student-oriented optimization，把教师奖励作为样本级权重，降低不可靠教师样本对学生蒸馏 loss 的影响。与普通 OPSD 相比，它不是假设教师总是对，而是把教师能力作为训练对象和过滤信号。","experiments":"实验覆盖不同参数规模的预训练 LLM，以及多个可验证任务。论文称 TESD 稳定优于代表性 OPSD 方法和序列级 RLVR 方法，并且能缓解 OPSD 训练中性能早停或波动的问题。全文片段提到作者还分析了自教师对学生 rollout 的置信度与 RLVR provides sparse sequence-level rewards, while OPSD tries to convert privileged context into dense token-level supervision. TESD shows that dense supervision is only useful when the teacher signal is reliable. Otherwise, distillation can efficiently amplify wrong preferences. The useful idea is to treat the self-teacher as a trainable role, not as a fixed source of trusted targets.","technical_approach":"TESD extends standard OPSD. In OPSD, the student samples on-policy rollouts, and a self-teacher conditioned on the query plus privileged context produces per-token distributions over the student trajectory; the student minimizes a divergence such as KL or JS against these distributions. TESD lets the self-teacher also generate rollouts and receive feedback. The reward combines task correctness with context independence, aiming to measure whether the teacher can produce correct solutions that are not merely tied to or copied from the privileged context. The framework then uses this reward in two ways: teacher-oriented optimization improves the model under the teacher-conditioned distribution, and adaptive student-oriented optimization uses the reward as a sample-wise weight to downweight distillation losses from unreliable teacher guidance.","experiments":"The experiments are run on pretrained LLMs of different sizes and multiple verifiable reasoning tasks. Once a search harness can write lexical queries, inspect results, reformulate, and decide when to stop, the limiting factor is no longer just vector indexing or Recall@k. The harder problem is how to compile a corpus before query time into compact, provenance-bound context that the harness can use efficiently.","importance":"The relevance to LLM pretraining is indirect. The useful signal is for agentic retrieval, long-context usage, and evaluation design. A strong harness changes what retrieval infrastructure should optimize: static index quality alone is not enough, and dense retrieval can fail to add value when the agent already performs semantic query reformulation and evidence reading. The negative results are the main contribution for researchers building RAG evaluations or training retrieval-aware agents.","technical_approach":"The paper defines knowledge compilation as a query-independent ingestion layer that transforms a corpus into typed, versioned artifacts with lineage links to source spans. The artifact family includes RAPTOR-style trees, PageIndex-style outlines, LLM wikis, entity-relation graphs, timelines, mind maps, ontology labels, claim-evidence units, and durable memory. The central argument is that these structures are not automatically extra evidence. Their role is to supply better context: smaller, traceable, and sufficient for a given question. The most concrete mechanism is claim-evidence compilation, where documents are preprocessed into atomic claims tied to verbatim evidence. The paper describes serving modes in which compiled claims replace, supplement, or filter raw passages, and develops a cost model for when this substitution pays off.","experiments":"The quantitative setting is English text QA with BM25/grep tools and an agentic harness. Structural artifacts such as summary trees, outlines, wikis, and entity-relation graphs show no detectable accuracy gain on FRAMES, and a PageIndex-style table-of-contents index can become harmful at FRAMES scale. For claim-evidence compilation, the paper reports a 2% break-even sufficiency rate, a 43% implied FRAMES rate, an amortization rule, and the latency cost of substituting compiled context for raw passages. Deployment artifacts such as ontologies, graphs, timelines, and memory are evaluated on separate instrumentation metrics rather than presented as controlled accuracy gains.","reading_guide":"Skim the introduction, the definition of knowledge compilation, the negative results on structural artifacts, and the claim-evidence cost model.
